Evidências: ferramentas de escrita do MCP openGym

a) Resumo executivo

O servidor MCP (mcp/), antes somente leitura, passa a escrever no perfil pela API HTTP do próprio openGym (PUT /api/data com baseRev, stamped:false e uma repetição em conflito 409), com 26 ferramentas registradas (9 de leitura já existentes e 17 novas), instruções para a LLM e o recurso opengym://guide.

Pegada no código existente

Arquivos pré-existentes alterados (todos de forma aditiva): CLAUDE.md, CONTRIBUTING.md, docs/README.md, mcp/README.md, mcp/package.json, mcp/scripts/check-node-loadable.mjs, mcp/src/index.js, mcp/src/state.js. Sobre as 10 falhas da API: são pré-existentes neste ambiente e não têm relação com o trabalho do MCP. A suíte completa foi executada de novo num worktree limpo da main, no mesmo ambiente: 596 testes, 583 passaram, 10 falharam, 3 pulados — exatamente as mesmas asserções. Todas estão nos testes do Coach de IA (coach-limits.test.js, jobs.test.js, payload-bounds.test.js, payload.test.js, routes.test.js: limites de fila, propostas/jobs, payload da revisão, debrief de coorte). A branch não altera nenhum arquivo em api/ (git diff --stat main...HEAD -- api é vazio), portanto não as causa nem as corrige.

As 10 asserções que falham (nesta branch e na main)
• the instance cap is reserved at enqueue, so a job still running already counts
• a proposal nobody has answered is not replaced by the next scheduled review
• an expired proposal disappears on read rather than lingering forever
• a runtime that cannot be cancelled still has its late answer discarded after a forget
• a review job produces a checked change-set, and nothing is left unvalidated
• the one repair round rescues an answer the validator rejected
• an id is cut, a policy outside the engine's five and a number that is not one read as absent
• the plan and the log a real app writes pass through exactly as before
• review payload carries the plan, the window, effort and aggregates
• a debrief is enqueued as its own kind, and the cohort routes gate on the admin switch and the opt-in

b) Catálogo das ferramentas

Descrição, esquema de entrada (extraído do tools/list do servidor real) e, quando capturada, uma chamada de exemplo com o resultado. As ferramentas marcadas existente não foram alteradas.

list_routines existente (leitura)

List the workout routines saved in the user's openGym profile (the same list the Plan screen shows). Each routine is a named set of exercises with set/rep targets. Use this to discover the plan structure before diving into a specific routine or today's workout.

Sem parâmetros.

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: nenhum

get_routine existente (leitura)

Get the full exercise list for a single routine (the same view the routine editor shows). Returns mode (reps/time/cardio), set/rep/weight targets (a `pyramid` list of per-set rep targets, 'max' meaning as many reps as possible, when the exercise uses pyramid sets, with `pyramid_rest_sec` and `pyramid_weight` per set where planned, 0 meaning the exercise's rest or last time's weight), superset links, any per-exercise custom increment or Epley deload factor, and each exercise's own rest in seconds (absent means it inherits the global rest timer). Use routine_id from list_routines.

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
routine_idstringsim

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: nenhum

preview_session existente (leitura)

Preview the session a routine will actually open with — the numbers the user will see after the progression policy and their training history have overridden the routine's own targets. This is NOT the same as get_routine: a routine storing "squat 3x8 @ 60kg" can open at 75kg because the policy progressed or deloaded from that routine's last logged session. The reps are the routine's own unless a policy that moves reps moved them, or the profile starts planned sessions from the last session (starts_from). Always call this (not get_routine) before telling someone what weight they are about to lift, or before judging whether an edit to a routine had any effect. Returns, per exercise, the planned target, the policy's decision and its stated reason, the opening set rows, and where each number came from. Defaults to today's scheduled routine.

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
routine_idstringnãoRoutine to preview. Defaults to the routine scheduled for `date`.
datestringnãoDate the session would be started on, YYYY-MM-DD. Affects which routine is scheduled and any one-off day override. Defaults to today.

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: nenhum

get_week_plan existente (leitura)

Show the user's training plan. `days` is the authoritative answer to "what is planned when": the next seven dates from today with the routines planned for each (a date can hold several — a combined day) and how each was decided: a coach-week session ("coach"), a session the user pinned to that date ("pinned"), a one-off routine override ("override"), a "rest" override ("rest_override"), the weekday plan ("weekday") or nothing ("rest"). `coach_week` is the current coach week when one is running (a week written by an external planner through the API, never by the app): its sessions are done IN ORDER on whatever days the user trains (no weekday attached), the first undone one is today's session, a session can be pinned to a date, and `waiting` means the week starts on `starts_on`. `weekdays` is the user's own weekly plan keyed by JS getDay() (Sunday=0 … Saturday=6, the openGym state convention) — in a coach week it holds only the routines the user planned themselves, which ride along beside the coach-week session.

Sem parâmetros.

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: nenhum

list_workouts existente (leitura)

List recent finished workouts, newest first. Each item summarises the date, exercise count, sets done / planned, total volume (in the user's unit), duration and whether PRs were set. Use this before drilling into a specific date with get_workout.

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
fromstringnãoInclusive start date YYYY-MM-DD. Defaults to no lower bound (list most recent).
tostringnãoInclusive end date YYYY-MM-DD. Defaults to today.
limitinteger
≥ 1; ≤ 200		nãoMax items to return. Defaults to 25.

get_workout existente (leitura)

Get the full breakdown of one workout: every exercise, its mode (reps/time/cardio), the target, and per-set labels (e.g. "5 @ 60 kg", "1:30 · 20 kg"). Identify it by workout_id (from list_workouts) or by date. Use list_workouts first if you don't know either.

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
datestringnãoThe workout date as YYYY-MM-DD. If two sessions share that date, the answer lists them instead and asks for a workout_id.
workout_idstringnãoThe id from list_workouts. Preferred: it names one session even on a day with two.

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: nenhum

get_bodyweight existente (leitura)

Get the body-weight log: chronological weigh-ins with weights, current goal, deltas vs goal (signed positive = above goal), and a latest summary. Useful for "am I trending toward my weight goal?" questions.

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
fromstringnãoInclusive start date YYYY-MM-DD.
tostringnãoInclusive end date YYYY-MM-DD. Defaults to today.

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: nenhum

estimate_1rm existente (leitura)

Estimate one-rep max using Epley, Brzycki or Lombardi formulas. If an exercise_id is given, returns the all-time best estimate for that exercise with the source set (weight × reps + date) and the trend across history. If no exercise_id is given, returns a PR table across all reps-mode exercises (sorted highest first). Refuses to guess above 12 reps — above that, formulas diverge past 10% and "work capacity" is read instead of "maximal strength".

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
exercise_idstringnãoAn exercise id from list_routines or get_workout entries. If omitted, returns a full PR table.
formulastring
um de: epley, brzycki, lombardi		nãoFormula to use. Defaults to epley.

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: nenhum

muscle_balance existente (leitura)

Show which muscles the user has trained in a period, ranked by "effective sets" (volume in kg is intentionally not used — 100 kg leg press vs 12 kg lateral raise say nothing about which muscle worked harder). Reports worked muscles with a 0-4 relative level (1 = some work, 4 = most worked) and the muscles trained zero times in that period — useful for "what am I neglecting?" questions.

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
periodstring
um de: week, month, all		simwindow: last 7 days, last 30 days, or all-time

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: nenhum

search_exercises nova (escrita)

Find exercises by name — in English, in the profile's language (e.g. Portuguese: "supino", "voador") or by id — across the 1,300+ built-in exercises and the profile's custom ones. CALL THIS FIRST whenever the user names an exercise in their own words, before log_workout, update_workout, last_performance, set_exercise_note or edit_routine: those tools accept names but fail with EAMBIG when several exercises match, and they never guess. Returns the best matches first with id, English name, localized name, body part, equipment and target muscle; pass the id on. Not for listing what a routine contains (get_routine) or what was trained (list_workouts). Read-only, works in every mode.

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
querystringsimName or part of it, any language the profile uses, or an exact exercise id.
body_partstring
um de: back, cardio, chest, full body, lower arms, lower legs, neck, shoulders, upper arms, upper legs, waist		nãoNarrow to a body part (catalogue values, e.g. "chest", "upper legs", "back").
equipmentstringnãoNarrow to an equipment value (e.g. "barbell", "dumbbell", "cable", "leverage machine", "body weight").
limitinteger
≥ 1; ≤ 25		nãoMax results, default 8.

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: EAMBIG

log_workout nova (escrita)

Save a FINISHED workout in one call — the primary way to record training: today's session or a past day, with every exercise and set. Use it when the user reports a session they have done ("fiz supino 3×10 com 60 kg e remada 4×8 com 50"). Do NOT use it while a live session started with start_workout is open (finish_workout or discard_workout first; the tool refuses with EINVAL). Resolve exercise names with search_exercises first; a name that matches several exercises fails with EAMBIG. Weights are the profile unit (see get_capabilities), totals on the bar or stack; dates YYYY-MM-DD; times HH:MM. Pass gym when the user says where they trained — it is stored on the workout and shown at the start of its note, and last_performance can filter by it. A past date is filed into history the way the app logs a past workout (badges rebuilt, kept weights untouched); today's is filed like a live session (PRs awarded, kept weights raised, the routine's progression advanced). Returns the record read back from the server: workout_id, volume, PRs, each exercise's sets, and warnings (e.g. a set given in plates with no kg factor on the exercise note). Relay workout_id and the numbers to the user. Requires remote mode (EPERM otherwise). Errors: ENOENT (not found), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — the error lists candidates; ask the user or pick, never guess), EPERM (writes need remote mode: OPENGYM_URL + OPENGYM_TOKEN), EAUTH (token refused — pair again), ECONFLICT (another device wrote at the same moment — retry once), ENET (server unreachable), EIO (server fault).

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
datestringnãoYYYY-MM-DD. Default today.
routinestringnãoRoutine id or name to link the session to (names it and feeds its progression). Omit for a freestyle session.
namestringnãoSession name. Default: the routine's name, else "Workout".
gymstringnãoWhere it was done, free text ("Iron", "Smart Fit Penha"). Keep the spelling consistent; get_capabilities lists gyms already on record.
startstringnãoLocal start time HH:MM. Default: now minus the duration (today), or 18:00 (a past date).
duration_mininteger
≥ 1; ≤ 1440		nãoLength in minutes. Default 60.
notestringnãoSession note ("ombro incomodou").
exercisesarray
objeto[]: exercise, note, note_pin, sets		simThe exercises in the order they were done, each with its sets.
exercises[]
parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
exercisestringsimExercise id (preferred — from search_exercises) or a name in English or the profile language. An ambiguous name fails with EAMBIG.
notestringnãoNote on this exercise for this session (grip, machine, variation — "pegada aberta, máquina 3").
note_pinbooleannãotrue to show this note again the next time the exercise comes up.
setsarray
objeto[]: w, plates, r, sec, min, speed, rir, rpe, failure, warmup, note		simThe sets in order. Each needs r, sec or min.
exercises.sets[]
parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
wnumber
≥ 0		nãoLoad in the profile unit (kg or lb; see get_capabilities), the TOTAL on the bar or stack. Omit for body-weight work.
platesinteger
≥ 1		nãoStack plates instead of w, for a machine counted in plates. Needs the exercise note to say "1 placa = X kg" (set_exercise_note); otherwise the set is stored without a weight and a warning comes back. Not together with w.
rinteger
≥ 0		nãoReps completed.
secinteger
≥ 0		nãoSeconds held, for a timed set (plank, hang).
minnumber
≥ 0		nãoMinutes, for cardio.
speednumber
≥ 0		nãokm/h, for cardio (stored in km/h whatever the display unit).
rirnumber
≥ 0; ≤ 10		nãoReps in reserve (0 = failure). Not together with rpe.
rpenumber
≥ 1; ≤ 10		nãoRPE 1–10. Not together with rir.
failurebooleannãotrue when the set was taken to failure.
warmupbooleannãotrue for a warm-up row: it is kept but excluded from volume, records and progression.
notestringnãoA note on this one set.

update_workout nova (escrita)

Correct a workout that is already saved: a set's weight or reps, a missing or extra set, the note, the name, the gym, the date, the start time or the duration ("na verdade foram 65 kg", "foram duas séries"). Find the id with list_workouts or get_workout first. `exercises`, when given, REPLACES every exercise and set of the workout — send the full corrected list, not just the changed set (read get_workout, apply the correction, send it all back). Not for a live session that is still open (use edit_set / delete_set). An edit that leaves no completed set is refused (EINVAL) — use delete_workout. Returns the corrected record read back from the server, plus `changed`. Requires remote mode. Errors: ENOENT (not found), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — the error lists candidates; ask the user or pick, never guess), EPERM (writes need remote mode: OPENGYM_URL + OPENGYM_TOKEN), EAUTH (token refused — pair again), ECONFLICT (another device wrote at the same moment — retry once), ENET (server unreachable), EIO (server fault).

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
workout_idstringsimThe id from list_workouts / get_workout / log_workout.
exercisesarray
objeto[]: exercise, note, note_pin, sets		nãoThe complete corrected exercise list; replaces all exercises and sets.
notestringnãoNew session note ("" clears it). The gym prefix is kept automatically.
namestringnãoNew session name.
gymstringnãoNew gym ("" clears it); the note prefix follows.
datestringnãoMove the workout to this date (YYYY-MM-DD).
startstringnãoNew local start time HH:MM.
duration_mininteger
≥ 1; ≤ 1440		nãoNew length in minutes.
exercises[]
parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
exercisestringsimExercise id (preferred — from search_exercises) or a name in English or the profile language. An ambiguous name fails with EAMBIG.
notestringnãoNote on this exercise for this session (grip, machine, variation — "pegada aberta, máquina 3").
note_pinbooleannãotrue to show this note again the next time the exercise comes up.
setsarray
objeto[]: w, plates, r, sec, min, speed, rir, rpe, failure, warmup, note		simThe sets in order. Each needs r, sec or min.
exercises.sets[]
parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
wnumber
≥ 0		nãoLoad in the profile unit (kg or lb; see get_capabilities), the TOTAL on the bar or stack. Omit for body-weight work.
platesinteger
≥ 1		nãoStack plates instead of w, for a machine counted in plates. Needs the exercise note to say "1 placa = X kg" (set_exercise_note); otherwise the set is stored without a weight and a warning comes back. Not together with w.
rinteger
≥ 0		nãoReps completed.
secinteger
≥ 0		nãoSeconds held, for a timed set (plank, hang).

≥ 0		nãoMinutes, for cardio.

≥ 0		nãokm/h, for cardio (stored in km/h whatever the display unit).
rirnumber
≥ 0; ≤ 10		nãoReps in reserve (0 = failure). Not together with rpe.

≥ 1; ≤ 10		nãoRPE 1–10. Not together with rir.

notestringnãoA note on this one set.

delete_workout nova (escrita)

Delete a saved workout for good (a duplicate, a session logged by mistake). ASK THE USER before calling this and pass confirm: true. Find the id with list_workouts. A kept working weight that came only from this workout is lowered, as the app does. Every device picks the removal up on its next sync. Requires remote mode. Errors: ENOENT (not found), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — the error lists candidates; ask the user or pick, never guess), EPERM (writes need remote mode: OPENGYM_URL + OPENGYM_TOKEN), EAUTH (token refused — pair again), ECONFLICT (another device wrote at the same moment — retry once), ENET (server unreachable), EIO (server fault).

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
workout_idstringsimThe id from list_workouts.
confirmbooleansimMust be true: the user confirmed the deletion.

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: EAMBIG EAUTH ECONFLICT EINVAL EIO ENET ENOENT EPERM

last_performance nova (escrita)

What the user did last time on one exercise — "qual foi o último peso no voador?", "last time at Iron" — with the most recent sessions (date, gym, every set), the best weight ever, the best estimated 1RM, the standing exercise note (seat, pin, plate factor) and the last pinned session note. Pass gym to see only sessions logged at that gym (case-insensitive; the gyms on record are listed in gyms_seen). Resolve the exercise with search_exercises if the name is ambiguous (EAMBIG). For what the app would PRESCRIBE next, use preview_session instead. Read-only, works in every mode.

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
exercisestringsimExercise id (from search_exercises) or a name.
gymstringnãoOnly sessions done at this gym (as logged with log_workout / start_workout).
limitinteger
≥ 1; ≤ 20		nãoHow many sessions, newest first. Default 5.

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: EAMBIG

add_bodyweight nova (escrita)

Log a weigh-in: the user's body weight in the profile unit, today or on a given date. A second weigh-in on the same date replaces the first. Returns the entry, the previous weigh-in and the delta, and the distance to the goal weight when one is set. Not for lifting weights (log_workout). Requires remote mode. Errors: ENOENT (not found), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — the error lists candidates; ask the user or pick, never guess), EPERM (writes need remote mode: OPENGYM_URL + OPENGYM_TOKEN), EAUTH (token refused — pair again), ECONFLICT (another device wrote at the same moment — retry once), ENET (server unreachable), EIO (server fault).

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
weightnumbersimBody weight in the profile unit (kg or lb — see get_capabilities).
datestringnãoYYYY-MM-DD. Default today.

set_exercise_note nova (escrita)

Set the STANDING note of an exercise — the facts that are true every session: seat and pin settings, the machine, a grip, and the plate factor for a stack counted in plates. The app shows it on the exercise card every time. Plate convention: include "1 placa = 5 kg" (or "1 plate = 10 lb") and from then on sets given as `plates` on that exercise are stored with a weight. An empty note clears it. For a note about ONE session's exercise use log_workout's per-exercise note instead. Requires remote mode. Errors: ENOENT (not found), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — the error lists candidates; ask the user or pick, never guess), EPERM (writes need remote mode: OPENGYM_URL + OPENGYM_TOKEN), EAUTH (token refused — pair again), ECONFLICT (another device wrote at the same moment — retry once), ENET (server unreachable), EIO (server fault).

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
exercisestringsimExercise id (from search_exercises) or a name.
notestringsimThe note; "" clears it. Up to 500 characters.

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: EAMBIG EAUTH ECONFLICT EINVAL EIO ENET ENOENT EPERM

edit_routine nova (escrita)

Create a routine, change one (name, emoji, exercises, order, sets/reps/weight/rest targets, supersets) or delete one. To CHANGE a routine read get_routine first: `exercises`, when given, REPLACES the whole list in the order sent. A slot kept for the same exercise keeps the settings this tool does not know (weight when none is sent, increment, pyramid…), but takes the shape the numbers given say: `reps` alone clears a stored rep range, and sec / min / reps move the slot to that mode and drop the other modes' numbers. To CREATE, omit `routine` and give name + exercises. To DELETE, pass remove: true and confirm: true after asking the user. Not for a one-off change to a session (log_workout / edit_active_workout) and not for what the app will prescribe (preview_session). Resolve exercise names with search_exercises (EAMBIG otherwise). Returns the routine as get_routine shows it. Requires remote mode. Errors: ENOENT (not found), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — the error lists candidates; ask the user or pick, never guess), EPERM (writes need remote mode: OPENGYM_URL + OPENGYM_TOKEN), EAUTH (token refused — pair again), ECONFLICT (another device wrote at the same moment — retry once), ENET (server unreachable), EIO (server fault).

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
routinestringnãoRoutine id or name to change or remove. Omit to create a new one.
namestringnãoRoutine name (required when creating).
emojistringnãoAn emoji for the routine ("" clears it).
exercisesarray
objeto[]: exercise, sets, reps, reps_min, reps_max, sec, min, speed, weight, rest_sec, superset_group		nãoThe full exercise list in order (replaces the current one).
removebooleannãotrue to delete the routine (with confirm: true).
confirmbooleannãoMust be true with remove: the user confirmed.
exercises[]
parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
exercisestringsimExercise id (from search_exercises) or a name; EAMBIG when ambiguous.
setsinteger
≥ 1; ≤ 20		nãoPlanned sets (default 3).
repsinteger
≥ 1		nãoPlanned reps per set (default 10). With reps_min this is the top of the range.
reps_mininteger
≥ 1		nãoBottom of a rep range for double progression (e.g. 8 with reps 12 = "8–12").
reps_maxinteger
≥ 1		nãoTop of a rep range; same as reps when both given.
secinteger
≥ 1		nãoSeconds per set for a timed exercise (makes it a timed slot).
minnumber
≥ 1		nãoMinutes for a cardio slot.
speednumber
≥ 0		nãokm/h for a cardio slot.
weightnumber
≥ 0		nãoStarting weight in the profile unit; the progression policy moves it from there.
rest_secinteger
≥ 0		nãoRest after each set in seconds; omit to use the profile's rest timer.
superset_groupstringnãoAdjacent exercises with the same group label are a superset (e.g. "A" on two neighbours).

start_workout nova (sessão)

Open a LIVE session to log set by set as the user trains ("estou indo malhar", "vou fazer o treino 2"). Use it only for a workout happening now; a session already over goes in one log_workout call. From a routine (id or name; list_routines shows them) the opening sets are what the app would put on screen — the progression policy's prescription over the user's history, the same numbers preview_session reports — so you can tell the user the weight before the first set. Without a routine it is a freestyle session: add exercises with log_set (naming the exercise) or edit_active_workout. Pass gym when the user says where they are. Only one session can be open (EINVAL otherwise — finish_workout or discard_workout it). The session is kept in a file on this server, never on the phone; the phone's own running workout is separate. Returns the same view as get_active_workout. Works in file mode too; finishing needs remote mode. Errors: ENOENT (no open session, or no such exercise/set), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — candidates listed; ask, never guess), EPERM (finishing needs remote mode), EAUTH, ECONFLICT, ENET, EIO (server; the session is kept).

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
routinestringnãoRoutine id or name; omit for a freestyle session.
gymstringnãoWhere the user is training ("Iron"). Stored on the workout at finish; get_capabilities lists gyms on record.
namestringnãoSession name. Default: the routine's name, else "Workout".
datestringnãoYYYY-MM-DD, default today. A past date files the session as a past workout at finish.
startstringnãoLocal start time HH:MM. Default: now (today) or 18:00 (a past date).

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: EAMBIG EAUTH ECONFLICT EINVAL EIO ENET ENOENT EPERM

get_active_workout nova (sessão)

The open live session, if any: the current exercise and its next planned set (weight and reps to tell the user), every exercise with its planned and logged sets (1-based set indexes for edit_set / delete_set), sets done and remaining. Call it before log_set when you are not sure which exercise is current, to answer "qual o próximo?" or "quantas faltam?", and after a correction to confirm. Returns { open: false } when nothing is open — then start_workout, or log_workout for a session already over. Read-only; works in every mode.

Sem parâmetros.

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: nenhum

log_set nova (sessão)

Record ONE set of the open live session ("fiz 12 com 45 kg", "mais 12", "subi para 50"). It ticks the next planned set of the exercise with the numbers given — a number not given keeps the planned one, so "fiz 12" at the planned weight only needs r — or appends a set when the plan's are all done. Without `exercise` it logs on the current exercise (get_active_workout shows which); naming an exercise that is not in the session adds it at the end. Weights in the profile unit, totals on the bar or stack; plates need the exercise note's factor (set_exercise_note) or the set is stored without a weight and a warning comes back. Returns the row as stored, the sets left on that exercise, the next planned set and, when the exercise is complete, the next exercise. A planned per-side (unilateral) set takes the numbers for both limbs together — give an even rep count, split evenly, EINVAL otherwise; a planned drop-set or rest-pause set keeps its drops/bursts when ticked without numbers, and is stored as a plain set (with a warning) when w or r is given. Needs an open session (start_workout; ENOENT otherwise). To change a set already logged use edit_set; to remove it delete_set. Errors: ENOENT (no open session, or no such exercise/set), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — candidates listed; ask, never guess), EPERM (finishing needs remote mode), EAUTH, ECONFLICT, ENET, EIO (server; the session is kept).

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
exercisestringnãoExercise id or name. Default: the current exercise of the session.
wnumber
≥ 0		nãoLoad in the profile unit, the TOTAL on the bar or stack. Omit to keep the planned weight (or for body-weight work).
platesinteger
≥ 1		nãoStack plates instead of w; stored as a weight only when the exercise note says "1 placa = X kg" (set_exercise_note), otherwise noted without a weight and a warning comes back. Not with w.

≥ 0		nãoReps completed. Omit to keep the planned reps.

failurebooleannãotrue when the set was taken to failure.

edit_set nova (sessão)

Change one set of the open live session ("a última foi com outro peso", "não, foram 10"): the fields given replace those of the set; the rest stay. Sets are 1-based per exercise, as get_active_workout lists them; the exercise defaults to the current one. A planned set that was not logged yet can be edited too (it stays unlogged until log_set). For a workout already saved use update_workout instead. Errors: ENOENT (no open session, or no such exercise/set), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — candidates listed; ask, never guess), EPERM (finishing needs remote mode), EAUTH, ECONFLICT, ENET, EIO (server; the session is kept).

delete_set nova (sessão)

Remove one set from the open live session ("não, foram duas séries" → delete the third). Sets are 1-based per exercise (get_active_workout); the exercise defaults to the current one. A planned set not yet logged is removed too, which shortens the plan for this session only. For a workout already saved use update_workout. Errors: ENOENT (no open session, or no such exercise/set), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — candidates listed; ask, never guess), EPERM (finishing needs remote mode), EAUTH, ECONFLICT, ENET, EIO (server; the session is kept).

set_indexinteger
≥ 1		simWhich set of that exercise, 1-based.

edit_active_workout nova (sessão)

Change the exercises of the open live session: `skip` removes an exercise that has no logged set ("não quero fazer esse" — delete its sets first if any were logged), `replace` swaps an exercise for another (`with`; an exercise with logged sets keeps them and the replacement is inserted after it), `move` moves it up or down one place ("não estou seguindo a ordem"), `add` appends an exercise with optional sets/reps/weight (or sec, or min). Exercises by id or name (search_exercises; EAMBIG when ambiguous). Returns the updated session view. For the saved plan itself use edit_routine. Errors: ENOENT (no open session, or no such exercise/set), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — candidates listed; ask, never guess), EPERM (finishing needs remote mode), EAUTH, ECONFLICT, ENET, EIO (server; the session is kept).

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
actionstring
um de: skip, replace, move, add		simWhat to do.
exercisestringsimThe exercise concerned (id or name): the one to skip, replace or move, or the one to add.
withstringnãoreplace: the exercise to put in its place (id or name).
directionstring
um de: up, down		nãomove: which way.
setsinteger
≥ 1; ≤ 20		nãoadd: planned sets (default: last time's, else 3).
repsinteger
≥ 1		nãoadd: planned reps per set.
weightnumber
≥ 0		nãoadd: planned weight in the profile unit.
secinteger
≥ 1		nãoadd: seconds per set for a timed exercise.
minnumber
≥ 1		nãoadd: minutes for cardio.

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: EAMBIG EAUTH ECONFLICT EINVAL EIO ENET ENOENT EPERM

finish_workout nova (sessão)

Close the open live session and SAVE it as a workout ("terminei, pode gravar"), through the same path log_workout uses: PRs awarded, the routine's progression advanced, every device picks it up on its next sync. Only sets that were logged count; planned sets left unticked are kept as planned-not-done, as the app does. Returns the record read back from the server (workout_id, volume, PRs, every set) — relay the id and the numbers, and the warnings. Refuses with EINVAL when no set was logged (discard_workout instead). Needs remote mode (EPERM) — on any failure the session is KEPT and finish_workout can be retried; a retry after a save that did land answers the saved record (already_saved: true) and closes the session, never a second record. Errors: ENOENT (no open session, or no such exercise/set), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — candidates listed; ask, never guess), EPERM (finishing needs remote mode), EAUTH, ECONFLICT, ENET, EIO (server; the session is kept).

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
notestringnãoSession note ("ombro incomodou"); the gym is prefixed automatically.
duration_mininteger
≥ 1; ≤ 1440		nãoLength in minutes. Default: from the session start to the last logged set — or to now, when that set was within the last 20 minutes — for today; 60 for a past date.

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: EAMBIG EAUTH ECONFLICT EINVAL EIO ENET ENOENT EPERM

discard_workout nova (sessão)

Drop the open live session WITHOUT saving anything. ASK THE USER before calling this and pass confirm: true. Nothing reaches the server; sets already logged in it are lost. To keep what was logged use finish_workout instead. Errors: ENOENT (no open session, or no such exercise/set), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — candidates listed; ask, never guess), EPERM (finishing needs remote mode), EAUTH, ECONFLICT, ENET, EIO (server; the session is kept).

parâmetrotipoobrig.descrição
confirmbooleansimMust be true: the user confirmed.

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: EAMBIG EAUTH ECONFLICT EINVAL EIO ENET ENOENT EPERM

Chamada capturada (file): {"confirm":true} → ok
{
  "ok": true,
  "discarded": {
    "started_at": "2026-10-23T02:57:14.547Z",
    "name": "Push Day",
    "gym": "Iron",
    "done_sets": 2,
    "exercises": 6
  }
}

get_capabilities nova (capacidades)

Call this FIRST in a conversation. Describes this openGym profile and what this server can do: whether writes are enabled (and why not), the profile's unit and language, counts, the gyms already on record, whether a live session is open, every tool grouped read / write / session with a one-line use, the recommended workflows (log a finished workout, live session set by set, correct a logged set, last weight at a gym, plates machines) and the known limitations. No arguments. Read-only.

Sem parâmetros.

Códigos de erro citados na descrição: nenhum

c) Instruções do servidor e guia

Texto que todo cliente MCP recebe no handshake (instructions) e o recurso opengym://guide (markdown, 22.2 KB), ambos lidos do servidor em execução.

instructions
openGym MCP server — a self-hosted gym log. One profile. Everything you read or write here is
that person's own training data.

Start every conversation by calling get_capabilities: it says whether writes are enabled, the
unit (kg or lb), the language, the gyms already on record, and whether a live session is open.

Rules:
- Resolve exercises before writing: call search_exercises with the user's words (any language);
  a tool that gets an ambiguous name fails with EAMBIG and a candidate list — ask, do not guess.
- Weights are in the profile unit, totals on the bar or stack. Dates are YYYY-MM-DD, times
  HH:MM. A set is one row: w, r (reps), sec (timed), min + speed (cardio), rir or rpe, failure.
- Dates and times are read in THIS SERVER's time zone (get_capabilities → clock.timezone and
  clock.today). When it is not the user's zone, name the date explicitly rather than trusting
  "today" late in the evening, and say the start time in the server's zone.
- A finished session goes in one log_workout call. Use the session tools (start_workout,
  log_set, finish_workout) only when the user is reporting set by set as they train.
- To fix a saved workout use update_workout with the full corrected exercise list; to fix a
  set of the open session use edit_set. Ask before delete_workout and discard_workout.
- "Where" matters to this user: pass gym on log_workout / start_workout when they say it, and
  use last_performance with gym to answer "last time at X".
- preview_session, not get_routine, says what weight the app would prescribe today.
- add_bodyweight logs a weigh-in; set_exercise_note keeps the standing facts of an exercise
  (seat, pin, "1 placa = 5 kg"); edit_routine changes the plan — read get_routine first and send
  the full exercise list.
- Every write returns the record read back from the server with its id and rev; relay the id
  and the numbers, and say when a warning came back (e.g. plates without a kg factor).
opengym://guide
# openGym MCP server — guide

openGym MCP server — a self-hosted gym log. One profile. Everything you read or write here is
that person's own training data.

Start every conversation by calling get_capabilities: it says whether writes are enabled, the
unit (kg or lb), the language, the gyms already on record, and whether a live session is open.

Rules:
- Resolve exercises before writing: call search_exercises with the user's words (any language);
  a tool that gets an ambiguous name fails with EAMBIG and a candidate list — ask, do not guess.
- Weights are in the profile unit, totals on the bar or stack. Dates are YYYY-MM-DD, times
  HH:MM. A set is one row: w, r (reps), sec (timed), min + speed (cardio), rir or rpe, failure.
- Dates and times are read in THIS SERVER's time zone (get_capabilities → clock.timezone and
  clock.today). When it is not the user's zone, name the date explicitly rather than trusting
  "today" late in the evening, and say the start time in the server's zone.
- A finished session goes in one log_workout call. Use the session tools (start_workout,
  log_set, finish_workout) only when the user is reporting set by set as they train.
- To fix a saved workout use update_workout with the full corrected exercise list; to fix a
  set of the open session use edit_set. Ask before delete_workout and discard_workout.
- "Where" matters to this user: pass gym on log_workout / start_workout when they say it, and
  use last_performance with gym to answer "last time at X".
- preview_session, not get_routine, says what weight the app would prescribe today.
- add_bodyweight logs a weigh-in; set_exercise_note keeps the standing facts of an exercise
  (seat, pin, "1 placa = 5 kg"); edit_routine changes the plan — read get_routine first and send
  the full exercise list.
- Every write returns the record read back from the server with its id and rev; relay the id
  and the numbers, and say when a warning came back (e.g. plates without a kg factor).

## Workflows

- **log_finished_workout**: search_exercises for anything not already an id → log_workout with every exercise and set (and gym) → relay workout_id, volume and prs from the result
- **live_session**: start_workout → get_active_workout when unsure what is next → log_set per set → edit_set / delete_set to correct → finish_workout
- **correct_logged_set**: list_workouts or get_workout to find workout_id → update_workout with the full corrected exercises list
- **last_weight_at_gym**: last_performance with exercise and gym
- **plates_machine**: set_exercise_note with "1 placa = X kg" once → then log sets as { plates: n }

## Limitations

- gym is stored on the workout and in its note; the app's screens do not show it as a field
- dates and times are in this server's time zone (clock.timezone); set TZ on the server to the user's zone
- a profile that never synced from a device has no document: the first write creates it with the app's defaults (unit kg) and says so in warnings
- a set given in plates is stored with a weight only when the exercise note carries "1 placa = X kg"
- writes are refused in file mode (set OPENGYM_URL and OPENGYM_TOKEN)
- an in-progress session lives on this server only; the phone's own running workout is separate — finishing both for the same training makes two records (delete_workout fixes it)

## Read tools

### `list_routines`

List the workout routines saved in the user's openGym profile (the same list the Plan screen shows). Each routine is a named set of exercises with set/rep targets. Use this to discover the plan structure before diving into a specific routine or today's workout.

### `get_routine`

Get the full exercise list for a single routine (the same view the routine editor shows). Returns mode (reps/time/cardio), set/rep/weight targets (a `pyramid` list of per-set rep targets, 'max' meaning as many reps as possible, when the exercise uses pyramid sets, with `pyramid_rest_sec` and `pyramid_weight` per set where planned, 0 meaning the exercise's rest or last time's weight), superset links, any per-exercise custom increment or Epley deload factor, and each exercise's own rest in seconds (absent means it inherits the global rest timer). Use routine_id from list_routines.

### `preview_session`

Preview the session a routine will actually open with — the numbers the user will see after the progression policy and their training history have overridden the routine's own targets. This is NOT the same as get_routine: a routine storing "squat 3x8 @ 60kg" can open at 75kg because the policy progressed or deloaded from that routine's last logged session. The reps are the routine's own unless a policy that moves reps moved them, or the profile starts planned sessions from the last session (starts_from). Always call this (not get_routine) before telling someone what weight they are about to lift, or before judging whether an edit to a routine had any effect. Returns, per exercise, the planned target, the policy's decision and its stated reason, the opening set rows, and where each number came from. Defaults to today's scheduled routine.

### `get_week_plan`

Show the user's training plan. `days` is the authoritative answer to "what is planned when": the next seven dates from today with the routines planned for each (a date can hold several — a combined day) and how each was decided: a coach-week session ("coach"), a session the user pinned to that date ("pinned"), a one-off routine override ("override"), a "rest" override ("rest_override"), the weekday plan ("weekday") or nothing ("rest"). `coach_week` is the current coach week when one is running (a week written by an external planner through the API, never by the app): its sessions are done IN ORDER on whatever days the user trains (no weekday attached), the first undone one is today's session, a session can be pinned to a date, and `waiting` means the week starts on `starts_on`. `weekdays` is the user's own weekly plan keyed by JS getDay() (Sunday=0 … Saturday=6, the openGym state convention) — in a coach week it holds only the routines the user planned themselves, which ride along beside the coach-week session.

### `list_workouts`

List recent finished workouts, newest first. Each item summarises the date, exercise count, sets done / planned, total volume (in the user's unit), duration and whether PRs were set. Use this before drilling into a specific date with get_workout.

### `get_workout`

Get the full breakdown of one workout: every exercise, its mode (reps/time/cardio), the target, and per-set labels (e.g. "5 @ 60 kg", "1:30 · 20 kg"). Identify it by workout_id (from list_workouts) or by date. Use list_workouts first if you don't know either.

### `get_bodyweight`

Get the body-weight log: chronological weigh-ins with weights, current goal, deltas vs goal (signed positive = above goal), and a latest summary. Useful for "am I trending toward my weight goal?" questions.

### `estimate_1rm`

Estimate one-rep max using Epley, Brzycki or Lombardi formulas. If an exercise_id is given, returns the all-time best estimate for that exercise with the source set (weight × reps + date) and the trend across history. If no exercise_id is given, returns a PR table across all reps-mode exercises (sorted highest first). Refuses to guess above 12 reps — above that, formulas diverge past 10% and "work capacity" is read instead of "maximal strength".

### `muscle_balance`

Show which muscles the user has trained in a period, ranked by "effective sets" (volume in kg is intentionally not used — 100 kg leg press vs 12 kg lateral raise say nothing about which muscle worked harder). Reports worked muscles with a 0-4 relative level (1 = some work, 4 = most worked) and the muscles trained zero times in that period — useful for "what am I neglecting?" questions.

## Write tools

### `search_exercises`

Find exercises by name — in English, in the profile's language (e.g. Portuguese: "supino", "voador") or by id — across the 1,300+ built-in exercises and the profile's custom ones. CALL THIS FIRST whenever the user names an exercise in their own words, before log_workout, update_workout, last_performance, set_exercise_note or edit_routine: those tools accept names but fail with EAMBIG when several exercises match, and they never guess. Returns the best matches first with id, English name, localized name, body part, equipment and target muscle; pass the id on. Not for listing what a routine contains (get_routine) or what was trained (list_workouts). Read-only, works in every mode.

### `log_workout`

Save a FINISHED workout in one call — the primary way to record training: today's session or a past day, with every exercise and set. Use it when the user reports a session they have done ("fiz supino 3×10 com 60 kg e remada 4×8 com 50"). Do NOT use it while a live session started with start_workout is open (finish_workout or discard_workout first; the tool refuses with EINVAL). Resolve exercise names with search_exercises first; a name that matches several exercises fails with EAMBIG. Weights are the profile unit (see get_capabilities), totals on the bar or stack; dates YYYY-MM-DD; times HH:MM. Pass gym when the user says where they trained — it is stored on the workout and shown at the start of its note, and last_performance can filter by it. A past date is filed into history the way the app logs a past workout (badges rebuilt, kept weights untouched); today's is filed like a live session (PRs awarded, kept weights raised, the routine's progression advanced). Returns the record read back from the server: workout_id, volume, PRs, each exercise's sets, and warnings (e.g. a set given in plates with no kg factor on the exercise note). Relay workout_id and the numbers to the user. Requires remote mode (EPERM otherwise). Errors: ENOENT (not found), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — the error lists candidates; ask the user or pick, never guess), EPERM (writes need remote mode: OPENGYM_URL + OPENGYM_TOKEN), EAUTH (token refused — pair again), ECONFLICT (another device wrote at the same moment — retry once), ENET (server unreachable), EIO (server fault).

### `update_workout`

Correct a workout that is already saved: a set's weight or reps, a missing or extra set, the note, the name, the gym, the date, the start time or the duration ("na verdade foram 65 kg", "foram duas séries"). Find the id with list_workouts or get_workout first. `exercises`, when given, REPLACES every exercise and set of the workout — send the full corrected list, not just the changed set (read get_workout, apply the correction, send it all back). Not for a live session that is still open (use edit_set / delete_set). An edit that leaves no completed set is refused (EINVAL) — use delete_workout. Returns the corrected record read back from the server, plus `changed`. Requires remote mode. Errors: ENOENT (not found), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — the error lists candidates; ask the user or pick, never guess), EPERM (writes need remote mode: OPENGYM_URL + OPENGYM_TOKEN), EAUTH (token refused — pair again), ECONFLICT (another device wrote at the same moment — retry once), ENET (server unreachable), EIO (server fault).

### `delete_workout`

Delete a saved workout for good (a duplicate, a session logged by mistake). ASK THE USER before calling this and pass confirm: true. Find the id with list_workouts. A kept working weight that came only from this workout is lowered, as the app does. Every device picks the removal up on its next sync. Requires remote mode. Errors: ENOENT (not found), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — the error lists candidates; ask the user or pick, never guess), EPERM (writes need remote mode: OPENGYM_URL + OPENGYM_TOKEN), EAUTH (token refused — pair again), ECONFLICT (another device wrote at the same moment — retry once), ENET (server unreachable), EIO (server fault).

### `last_performance`

What the user did last time on one exercise — "qual foi o último peso no voador?", "last time at Iron" — with the most recent sessions (date, gym, every set), the best weight ever, the best estimated 1RM, the standing exercise note (seat, pin, plate factor) and the last pinned session note. Pass gym to see only sessions logged at that gym (case-insensitive; the gyms on record are listed in gyms_seen). Resolve the exercise with search_exercises if the name is ambiguous (EAMBIG). For what the app would PRESCRIBE next, use preview_session instead. Read-only, works in every mode.

### `add_bodyweight`

Log a weigh-in: the user's body weight in the profile unit, today or on a given date. A second weigh-in on the same date replaces the first. Returns the entry, the previous weigh-in and the delta, and the distance to the goal weight when one is set. Not for lifting weights (log_workout). Requires remote mode. Errors: ENOENT (not found), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — the error lists candidates; ask the user or pick, never guess), EPERM (writes need remote mode: OPENGYM_URL + OPENGYM_TOKEN), EAUTH (token refused — pair again), ECONFLICT (another device wrote at the same moment — retry once), ENET (server unreachable), EIO (server fault).

### `set_exercise_note`

Set the STANDING note of an exercise — the facts that are true every session: seat and pin settings, the machine, a grip, and the plate factor for a stack counted in plates. The app shows it on the exercise card every time. Plate convention: include "1 placa = 5 kg" (or "1 plate = 10 lb") and from then on sets given as `plates` on that exercise are stored with a weight. An empty note clears it. For a note about ONE session's exercise use log_workout's per-exercise note instead. Requires remote mode. Errors: ENOENT (not found), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — the error lists candidates; ask the user or pick, never guess), EPERM (writes need remote mode: OPENGYM_URL + OPENGYM_TOKEN), EAUTH (token refused — pair again), ECONFLICT (another device wrote at the same moment — retry once), ENET (server unreachable), EIO (server fault).

### `edit_routine`

Create a routine, change one (name, emoji, exercises, order, sets/reps/weight/rest targets, supersets) or delete one. To CHANGE a routine read get_routine first: `exercises`, when given, REPLACES the whole list in the order sent. A slot kept for the same exercise keeps the settings this tool does not know (weight when none is sent, increment, pyramid…), but takes the shape the numbers given say: `reps` alone clears a stored rep range, and sec / min / reps move the slot to that mode and drop the other modes' numbers. To CREATE, omit `routine` and give name + exercises. To DELETE, pass remove: true and confirm: true after asking the user. Not for a one-off change to a session (log_workout / edit_active_workout) and not for what the app will prescribe (preview_session). Resolve exercise names with search_exercises (EAMBIG otherwise). Returns the routine as get_routine shows it. Requires remote mode. Errors: ENOENT (not found), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — the error lists candidates; ask the user or pick, never guess), EPERM (writes need remote mode: OPENGYM_URL + OPENGYM_TOKEN), EAUTH (token refused — pair again), ECONFLICT (another device wrote at the same moment — retry once), ENET (server unreachable), EIO (server fault).

## Session tools

### `start_workout`

Open a LIVE session to log set by set as the user trains ("estou indo malhar", "vou fazer o treino 2"). Use it only for a workout happening now; a session already over goes in one log_workout call. From a routine (id or name; list_routines shows them) the opening sets are what the app would put on screen — the progression policy's prescription over the user's history, the same numbers preview_session reports — so you can tell the user the weight before the first set. Without a routine it is a freestyle session: add exercises with log_set (naming the exercise) or edit_active_workout. Pass gym when the user says where they are. Only one session can be open (EINVAL otherwise — finish_workout or discard_workout it). The session is kept in a file on this server, never on the phone; the phone's own running workout is separate. Returns the same view as get_active_workout. Works in file mode too; finishing needs remote mode. Errors: ENOENT (no open session, or no such exercise/set), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — candidates listed; ask, never guess), EPERM (finishing needs remote mode), EAUTH, ECONFLICT, ENET, EIO (server; the session is kept).

### `get_active_workout`

The open live session, if any: the current exercise and its next planned set (weight and reps to tell the user), every exercise with its planned and logged sets (1-based set indexes for edit_set / delete_set), sets done and remaining. Call it before log_set when you are not sure which exercise is current, to answer "qual o próximo?" or "quantas faltam?", and after a correction to confirm. Returns { open: false } when nothing is open — then start_workout, or log_workout for a session already over. Read-only; works in every mode.

### `log_set`

Record ONE set of the open live session ("fiz 12 com 45 kg", "mais 12", "subi para 50"). It ticks the next planned set of the exercise with the numbers given — a number not given keeps the planned one, so "fiz 12" at the planned weight only needs r — or appends a set when the plan's are all done. Without `exercise` it logs on the current exercise (get_active_workout shows which); naming an exercise that is not in the session adds it at the end. Weights in the profile unit, totals on the bar or stack; plates need the exercise note's factor (set_exercise_note) or the set is stored without a weight and a warning comes back. Returns the row as stored, the sets left on that exercise, the next planned set and, when the exercise is complete, the next exercise. A planned per-side (unilateral) set takes the numbers for both limbs together — give an even rep count, split evenly, EINVAL otherwise; a planned drop-set or rest-pause set keeps its drops/bursts when ticked without numbers, and is stored as a plain set (with a warning) when w or r is given. Needs an open session (start_workout; ENOENT otherwise). To change a set already logged use edit_set; to remove it delete_set. Errors: ENOENT (no open session, or no such exercise/set), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — candidates listed; ask, never guess), EPERM (finishing needs remote mode), EAUTH, ECONFLICT, ENET, EIO (server; the session is kept).

### `edit_set`

Change one set of the open live session ("a última foi com outro peso", "não, foram 10"): the fields given replace those of the set; the rest stay. Sets are 1-based per exercise, as get_active_workout lists them; the exercise defaults to the current one. A planned set that was not logged yet can be edited too (it stays unlogged until log_set). For a workout already saved use update_workout instead. Errors: ENOENT (no open session, or no such exercise/set), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — candidates listed; ask, never guess), EPERM (finishing needs remote mode), EAUTH, ECONFLICT, ENET, EIO (server; the session is kept).

### `delete_set`

Remove one set from the open live session ("não, foram duas séries" → delete the third). Sets are 1-based per exercise (get_active_workout); the exercise defaults to the current one. A planned set not yet logged is removed too, which shortens the plan for this session only. For a workout already saved use update_workout. Errors: ENOENT (no open session, or no such exercise/set), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — candidates listed; ask, never guess), EPERM (finishing needs remote mode), EAUTH, ECONFLICT, ENET, EIO (server; the session is kept).

### `edit_active_workout`

Change the exercises of the open live session: `skip` removes an exercise that has no logged set ("não quero fazer esse" — delete its sets first if any were logged), `replace` swaps an exercise for another (`with`; an exercise with logged sets keeps them and the replacement is inserted after it), `move` moves it up or down one place ("não estou seguindo a ordem"), `add` appends an exercise with optional sets/reps/weight (or sec, or min). Exercises by id or name (search_exercises; EAMBIG when ambiguous). Returns the updated session view. For the saved plan itself use edit_routine. Errors: ENOENT (no open session, or no such exercise/set), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — candidates listed; ask, never guess), EPERM (finishing needs remote mode), EAUTH, ECONFLICT, ENET, EIO (server; the session is kept).

### `finish_workout`

Close the open live session and SAVE it as a workout ("terminei, pode gravar"), through the same path log_workout uses: PRs awarded, the routine's progression advanced, every device picks it up on its next sync. Only sets that were logged count; planned sets left unticked are kept as planned-not-done, as the app does. Returns the record read back from the server (workout_id, volume, PRs, every set) — relay the id and the numbers, and the warnings. Refuses with EINVAL when no set was logged (discard_workout instead). Needs remote mode (EPERM) — on any failure the session is KEPT and finish_workout can be retried; a retry after a save that did land answers the saved record (already_saved: true) and closes the session, never a second record. Errors: ENOENT (no open session, or no such exercise/set), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — candidates listed; ask, never guess), EPERM (finishing needs remote mode), EAUTH, ECONFLICT, ENET, EIO (server; the session is kept).

### `discard_workout`

Drop the open live session WITHOUT saving anything. ASK THE USER before calling this and pass confirm: true. Nothing reaches the server; sets already logged in it are lost. To keep what was logged use finish_workout instead. Errors: ENOENT (no open session, or no such exercise/set), EINVAL (bad input), EAMBIG (ambiguous name — candidates listed; ask, never guess), EPERM (finishing needs remote mode), EAUTH, ECONFLICT, ENET, EIO (server; the session is kept).

## Capabilities

### `get_capabilities`

Call this FIRST in a conversation. Describes this openGym profile and what this server can do: whether writes are enabled (and why not), the profile's unit and language, counts, the gyms already on record, whether a live session is open, every tool grouped read / write / session with a one-line use, the recommended workflows (log a finished workout, live session set by set, correct a logged set, last weight at a gym, plates machines) and the known limitations. No arguments. Read-only.

e) Revisão independente e defeitos do e2e

Onze achados do revisor independente e três do agente que escreveu o teste ponta a ponta. Todos corrigidos no commit 836ae2d5, cada um com teste de regressão.

#achadostatusteste de regressão
R1finish_workout não era idempotente: morte do processo entre o PUT e a limpeza do rascunho duplicava o treino com o mesmo idcorrigidomutations "a draft whose id is already filed…"; session-tools "a finish retried after its push landed…"
R2Chaves de protótipo ("constructor", "__proto__") resolviam como exercício e gravavam entrada sem idcorrigidoexercise-search "what the user typed is never a property lookup"; write-tools '"constructor" is not an exercise anywhere a name is taken'
R3Renovação do token só na primeira leitura: um Hermes de longa duração expiravacorrigidoremote "a long-running process asks GET /api/me again…"
R4Datas e horas no fuso do host (UTC em Docker): treino das 22h caía no dia seguintecorrigidocapabilities "the clock"; TZ documentado no README (sem mudança na aritmética de datas)
R5log_set achatava séries unilaterais (sides) e drop sets / rest-pause vindas do planocorrigidosession-tools "a planned per-side set…", "a planned drop-set keeps its drops…"
R6Avisos duplicados após a tentativa de novo em 409corrigidowrite-tools "a 409 retry does not double the warnings"
R7Perfil sem documento: add_bodyweight criava o documento e as outras escritas recusavam (inconsistente)corrigidowrite-tools "a profile with no document yet…"; session-tools "a profile with no document yet can run a session…"; e2e "log_workout on a profile with no document yet creates it and says so"
R8Treino de hoje com start explícito era anexado fora de ordem e podia terminar no futurocorrigidomutations "an explicit start on today… filed in time order", "a start with no length runs until now; a future start or end is EINVAL"
R9Cache podia guardar um documento que o servidor corrigiu (_rev + 2)corrigidowrite-tools "the cache is re-read on the next call after a write"
R10Arquivo de sessão com uid nulo se o primeiro GET /api/me falhassecorrigidosession-tools "remote mode with no profile yet resolved…"
R11Detalhes de esquema: confirm como literal true; descrição de finish_workout; semântica de update em edit_routine (peso zerado quando omitido); export getBackend sem usocorrigidomutations "updating keeps a stored weight, clears a rep range…"; session-tools description audit
E1Escrita recusada num perfil sem documento (conta recém-pareada)corrigidoe2e "log_workout on a profile with no document yet creates it and says so"
E2Busca localizada só no idioma do perfil: "supino com barra" não achava nada num perfil em inglêscorrigidoexercise-search "the any-language fallback"; write-tools "search_exercises on an English profile…"
E3update_workout.changed usava nomes diferentes dos campos de entradacorrigidoe2e "update_workout can move the date, retime and rename without touching the sets"

f) Limitações conhecidas e configuração

  • Academia: o app não tem esse campo; o MCP grava gym no treino (a sincronização preserva campos extras) e prefixa a observação, que o app mostra. Filtros por academia só pelo MCP.
  • Placas: a carga é gravada em kg/lb. Só converte placas quando a nota fixa do exercício traz o fator ("1 placa = 5 kg"); sem fator, registra a contagem na observação, sem carga, com aviso.
  • Fuso horário: datas e horas são lidas no fuso do processo do MCP. Configure TZ no cliente; get_capabilities reporta clock.timezone.
  • Modo arquivo (OPENGYM_DATA) continua somente leitura: toda escrita devolve EPERM. Escrita exige OPENGYM_URL + token.
  • Sessão ao vivo fica num arquivo local do MCP, nunca sincroniza; não misturar com uma sessão aberta no celular.
  • Perfil sem documento: a primeira escrita cria o documento com os padrões (kg) e avisa.
  • Busca em perfil em inglês: a busca por nomes em português cai para todos os pacotes de idioma; termos curtos ou ambíguos ("prancha", "supino reto com barra" num perfil sem histórico) podem voltar EAMBIG com candidatos — a LLM deve escolher pelo id. Com o perfil em pt-BR ou com histórico, o exercício já treinado tem prioridade.
  • CI: o teste e2e sobe api/server.js, então o job mcp do workflow precisa instalar as dependências de api/ (hoje só o job api faz isso).

Pareamento

# no app: Configurações → Parear celular → código de 5 minutos
cd mcp
OPENGYM_TOKEN_FILE=~/.opengym-mcp/token node src/index.js pair https://gym.example.com CODIGO

Hermes Agent (~/.hermes/config.yaml)

mcp_servers:
  opengym:
    command: node
    args: ["/absolute/path/to/openGym/mcp/src/index.js"]
    env:
      OPENGYM_URL: "https://gym.example.com"
      OPENGYM_TOKEN_FILE: "/home/you/.opengym-mcp/token"
      TZ: "America/Sao_Paulo"

Claude Desktop (modo arquivo; para escrita troque o env por OPENGYM_URL + OPENGYM_TOKEN_FILE)

{
  "mcpServers": {
    "opengym": {
      "command": "node",
      "args": ["/absolute/path/to/openGym/mcp/src/index.js"],
      "env": {
        "OPENGYM_DATA": "/absolute/path/to/openGym/data",
        "OPENGYM_UID": "<your-uid>",  // optional — auto-detected if you have one profile
        "TZ": "America/Sao_Paulo"     // the user's zone: dates and "today" are read in it
      }
    }
  }
}

g) Como reproduzir

git fetch origin feature/mcp-write-tools && git checkout feature/mcp-write-tools
cd api && npm ci --omit=optional && npm test          # suíte da API (o e2e do MCP precisa de api/node_modules)
cd ../mcp && npm ci && npm test                        # 10 arquivos, inclui test/e2e.test.js contra api/server.js
npm run check:node-loadable                            # carga em Node puro
cd ../frontend && npm ci && npm test                   # regressão do app (nenhum arquivo alterado)
# servidor real em modo arquivo (só leitura):
OPENGYM_DATA=/caminho/data node mcp/src/index.js
# modo remoto (leitura e escrita):
OPENGYM_URL=https://gym.example.com OPENGYM_TOKEN_FILE=~/.opengym-mcp/token TZ=America/Sao_Paulo node mcp/src/index.js

Documento de design: docs/dev/MCP_WRITE.md (com a seção "After review"). Guia do usuário: mcp/README.md.
